Which of the following treatments is absolutely contraindicated in this situation? 	صلي الله على محمد CardioThoracic PRO Dr._Mowafy	2	a) Captopril 	b) Metoprolol 	c) Nitroglycerin 	d) Tissue plasminogen activator (tPA)					0 0 1 0 0 0 0 0	The co-administration of nitrates within 24 hours after taking sildenafil in absolutely contraindicated.
